# FN-FP-007 — Financial Compliance & Fraud Monitoring Controls
## 1. Policy Header

| Field | Value |
| --- | --- |
| Policy ID | FN-FP-007 |
| Title | Financial Compliance & Fraud Monitoring Controls |
| Domain | FN — Finance & Revenue Cycle |
| Subdomain | FP — Financial Planning & Performance |
| Version | 6.0 |
| Effective Date | 2025-07-10 |
| Status | ACTIVE |
| Classification Tier | ESSENTIAL |
| Review Cycle | Annual |
| Next Review Date | 2026-07-10 |
| Last Reviewed | 2025-07-10 |
| Policy Owner/Steward | CFO / Revenue Cycle Director |
| Access Tier | Tier 3 — Confidential |
| Approved By | Governing Body Chair — Care Indeed Home Health Care, Inc. |
| Supersedes | N/A (Initial Version) |
| Regulatory Alignment | 42 CFR § 484.100, 31 U.S.C. §§ 3729–3733, 42 U.S.C. § 1320a-7b (AKS), 42 U.S.C. § 1320a-7k(d), OIG Compliance Program Guidance |

## 2. Purpose
This policy establishes the agency's financial monitoring controls for detecting, preventing, and reporting billing anomalies, fraudulent patterns, and aberrant financial activity. Effective financial fraud monitoring is a core requirement of the OIG Compliance Program Guidance for Home Health Agencies, which mandates that agencies implement internal controls to detect billing irregularities. This policy integrates billing pattern analysis, outlier detection, internal financial audits, and escalation procedures to ensure the agency identifies potential fraud, waste, and abuse at the earliest possible stage.
## 3. Scope
CFO / Revenue Cycle Director, Compliance Officer, Administrator, billing coordinators, certified coders, QAPI Coordinator.
## 4. Policy Statements
4.1 The agency shall maintain a financial fraud monitoring program that includes: (a) monthly billing pattern analysis; (b) outlier detection for utilization, coding, and payment patterns; (c) quarterly internal financial audits; (d) integration with the corporate compliance program (CO-CP-001) and FWA prevention program (CO-FA-003).
4.2 The Revenue Cycle Director and Compliance Officer shall jointly conduct monthly billing pattern analysis comparing agency metrics against national and state benchmarks.
4.3 The following billing patterns shall be monitored as potential fraud/abuse indicators:

| Indicator | Monitoring Metric | Trigger Threshold |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Average visits per episode | Agency avg vs. national avg | >20% above national average |
| PDGM case-mix weight | Agency avg vs. national avg | >15% above national average |
| LUPA rate | Agency rate vs. national avg | Significantly below national average (may indicate visit padding) |
| Therapy utilization | % episodes with therapy | >20% above national average |
| Diagnosis coding concentration | Top 10 diagnoses % of total | Unusual concentration in high-paying clinical groups |
| Admission source distribution | Institutional vs. community % | Significant deviation from regional norms |
| Referral source concentration | % from single referral source | >40% from any single source (AKS indicator) |
| Episode length | Avg days on service | >20% above national average |
| Clinician productivity | Visits per clinician per day | >8 visits/day (productivity may affect quality) |
| Staff-to-patient ratio | Clinician caseload | Unusual ratios |

4.4 Quarterly internal financial audits shall include: (a) sample of claims tested for documentation support; (b) referral relationship financial analysis (AKS screening per CO-FA-001); (c) clinician productivity analysis; (d) cost report accuracy review.
4.5 Any billing pattern or financial anomaly that may indicate fraud, abuse, or systematic improper billing shall be reported to the Compliance Officer within 24 hours for investigation per CO-CP-007.
4.6 Financial monitoring results shall be reported to the CFO monthly, the Compliance Officer monthly, and the Governing Body quarterly.
## 6. Procedures
### 6.1 Monthly Billing Pattern Analysis

| Step | Action | Responsible Party | Timeframe |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 6.1.1 | The Revenue Cycle Director shall produce the Monthly Financial Monitoring Report (Appendix A) comparing agency billing patterns against CMS benchmarks and prior periods. | Revenue Cycle Director | Monthly — by 20th of following month |
| 6.1.2 | The Compliance Officer shall review the report for patterns indicating potential FWA. | Compliance Officer | Within 5 business days |
| 6.1.3 | Any indicator exceeding its trigger threshold shall be flagged for investigation. The investigation shall determine whether the pattern reflects: (a) legitimate clinical/operational factors; (b) data/system error; (c) potential inappropriate billing or fraud. | Revenue Cycle Director + Compliance Officer | Within 14 calendar days of identification |
| 6.1.4 | Investigation findings shall be documented. If the pattern cannot be explained by legitimate factors, escalate per §6.3. | Compliance Officer | Upon investigation completion |

### 6.2 Quarterly Internal Financial Audit

| Step | Action | Responsible Party | Timeframe |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 6.2.1 | Select audit sample: minimum 5% of claims billed in the quarter (minimum 15 claims). | Compliance Officer | Quarterly |
| 6.2.2 | For each sampled claim: (a) verify all services billed were actually rendered; (b) verify documentation supports all billed services; (c) verify coding accuracy per FN-CM-001/002; (d) verify PDGM classification accuracy; (e) verify physician certification on file; (f) verify no duplicate billing. | Audit Lead | During audit |
| 6.2.3 | Analyze referral source financial data for AKS risk per CO-FA-001: (a) identify all referral sources; (b) review any financial arrangements with referral sources; (c) flag any referral source accounting for >40% of admissions. | Compliance Officer | During audit |
| 6.2.4 | Document findings in the Quarterly Financial Audit Report (Appendix B). | Audit Lead | Within 30 calendar days of quarter end |
| 6.2.5 | Report results to CFO, Compliance Officer, and Governing Body. | Compliance Officer | Quarterly |

### 6.3 Escalation Matrix

| Condition | Escalation Path | Timeframe |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Billing pattern exceeds trigger threshold | Revenue Cycle Director and Compliance Officer investigate | Within 14 calendar days |
| Pattern cannot be explained by legitimate factors | Compliance Officer initiates investigation per CO-CP-007 | Within 7 calendar days |
| Potential fraud identified | Compliance Officer, Administrator, CFO, legal counsel | Within 24 hours |
| Referral relationship with financial arrangement flagged | Compliance Officer and legal counsel review per CO-FA-001 | Within 30 calendar days |
| Financial audit reveals claim not supported by documentation | Compliance Officer evaluates for overpayment per FN-BC-004 | Within 24 hours |
| Governing Body requires briefing | CFO presents at next quarterly meeting or special meeting | Per GV-GB-001 |

## 7. Documentation Requirements

| Requirement | Document / Record | Responsible Party | Location | Retention |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Monthly analysis | Monthly Financial Monitoring Report (Appendix A) | Revenue Cycle Director | Financial/compliance records | 7 years |
| Quarterly audit | Quarterly Financial Audit Report (Appendix B) | Compliance Officer | Compliance/financial records | 7 years |
| Investigation files | Investigation documentation per CO-CP-007 | Compliance Officer | Compliance file (may be privileged) | 10 years |
| Policy acknowledgments | Signed Appendix C | Revenue Cycle Director | Policy acknowledgment file | 7 years |

## 8. Compliance Monitoring

| Compliance Indicator | Measurement Method | Acceptable Standard |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Monthly financial monitoring report completed | Report on file | 100% (12 per year) |
| Quarterly financial audit completed | Report on file | 100% (4 per year) |
| All flagged patterns investigated within 14 days | Investigation log | 100% |
| Potential fraud escalated within 24 hours | Investigation timeline | 100% |
| Financial monitoring results reported to Governing Body quarterly | GB minutes | 100% |
| Zero undetected systematic billing anomalies | Audit and monitoring outcomes | Zero |

## 9. References
42 CFR § 484.100, 31 U.S.C. §§ 3729–3733 (FCA), 42 U.S.C. § 1320a-7b (AKS), OIG Compliance Program Guidance.

## 10–11. Training and Version Control
Per standard format. Annual training on financial monitoring, FWA indicators, reporting obligations. Appendix C acknowledgment. EN-LC-001 version control.
